- 2.0 * center) * invdz2;\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Load a halo-padded tile of a scalar field into shared memory using clamped\n// (zero-flux) boundary conditions identical to the original code.\n__device__ __forceinline__ void loadTile(double (&s)[SZ][SY][SX],\n                                         const double* __restrict__ field,\n                                         const int bx, const int by, const int bz,\n                                         const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                         const int tid) {\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int i = tid; i < TILE_ELEMS; i += BLOCK_THREADS) {\n        const int lx = i % SX;\n        const int rem = i / SX;\n        const int ly = rem % SY;\n        const int lz = rem / SY;\n\n        int }\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\n__global__ void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                               const double* __restrict__ c,\n                                               const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                               const double invdx2, const double invdy2,\n double e_AB) {\n    __shared__ double s[SZ][SY][SX];\n\n    const int bx = blockIdx.x * BX;\n    const int by = blockIdx.y * BY;\n    const int bz = blockIdx.z * BZ;\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x + threadIdx.y * BX + threadIdx.z * (BX * BY);\n\n    loadTile(s, c, bx, by, bz, nx, ny, nz, tid);\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const int x = bx + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = by + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = bz + threadIdx.z;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz) return;\n\n    const int lx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ly = threadIdx.y + 1;\n    const int lz = threadIdx.z + 1;\n\n    const double cv = s[lz][ly][lx];\n    const double lap = laplacianFromTile(s, lx, ly, lz, invdx2, invdy2, invdz2);\n\n    const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * (static_cast<size_t>(ny) * nx)\n                     + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;\n\n    mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n            - gamma * lap;\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\n__global__ void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew,\n                                         const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                         const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                         const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                         const double dtD,\n                                         const double invdx2, const double invdy2,\n                                         const double invdz2) {\n    __shared__ double s[SZ][SY][SX];\n\n    const int bx = blockIdx.x * BX;\n    const int by = blockIdx.y * BY;\n    const int bz = blockIdx.z * BZ;\n    const int tid = threadIdx.x + threadIdx.y * BX + threadIdx.z * (BX * BY);\n\n    loadTile(s, mu, bx, by, bz, nx, ny, nz, tid);\n    __syncthreads();\n\n    const int x = bx + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = by + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = bz + threadIdx.z;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz) return;\n\n    const int lx = threadIdx.x + 1;\n    const int ly = threadIdx.y + 1;\n    const int lz = threadIdx.z + 1;\n\n    const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * (static_cast<size_t>(ny) * nx)\n                     + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;\n\n    const double lap = laplacianFromTile(s, lx, ly, lz, invdx2, invdy2, invdz2);\n    cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dtD * lap;\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {\n    // Check for NaN or Inf\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds\n    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: values out of expected range\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n  const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);\n    const double gamma = 0.5;\n    const double D = 1.0;\n\n    const double invdx2 = 1.0 / (dx * dx);\n    const double BX is a multiple of the warp size so that accesses\n// along the contiguous X dimension are fully coalesced.\nconstexpr int BX = 32;\nconstexpr int BY = 2;\nconstexpr int BZ = 2;\n\n// Compute the Laplacian directly from global memory. Neighbouring threads\n// share stencil values through the read-only/L1 cache, and the clamped\n// boundary conditions match the original scalar code exactly.\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianGlobal(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                                  const int x, const int y, const int z,\n                                                  const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                                  const double invdx2, const double invdy2,\n                                                  const double invdz2) {\n    const size_t stride_y = nx;\n    const size_t stride_z = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;\n    const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * stride_z\n                     + static_cast<size_t>(y) * stride_y + x;\n    const double center = f[idx];\n    const double xp = (x < nx - 1) ? f[idx + 1] : center;\n    const double xn = (x > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : center;\n    const double yp = (y < ny - 1) ? f[idx + stride_y] : center;\n    const double yn = (y > 0) ? f[idx - stride_y] : center;\n    const double zp = (z < nz -                                              \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                              \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error at %s:%d: %s\\n\", __FILE__, __LINE__,    \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                  \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                 \\\n        }                                                                       \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Thread-block dimensions. BX is a multiple of the warp size so that accesses\n// along the contiguous X dimension are fully coalesced.\nconstexpr int BX = 32;\nconstexpr int BY = 2;\nconstexpr int BZ = 2;\n\n// Compute the Laplacian directly from global memory. Neighbouring threads\n// share stencil values through the read-only/L1 cache, and the clamped\n// boundary conditions match the original scalar code exactly.\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianGlobal(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                                  const int x, const int y, const int z,\n                                                  const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                                  const double invdx2, const double invdy2,\n                                                  const double invdz2) {\n    const size_t stride_y = nx;\n    const size_t stride_z = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;\n    const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * stride_z\n                     + static_cast<size_t>(y) * stride_y + x;\n    const double center = f[idx];\n    const double xp = (x < nx - 1) ? f[idx + 1] : center;\n    const double xn = (x > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : center;\n    const double yp = (y < ny - 1) ? f[idx + stride_y] : center;\n    const double yn = (y > 0) ? f[idx - stride_y] : center;\n    const double zp = (z < nz - 1) ? f[idx + stride_z] : center;\n    const double zn = (z > 0) ? f[idx - stride_z] : center;\n    const double cxx = (xp + xn - 2.0 * center) * invdx2;\n    const double cyy = (yp + yn - 2.0 * center) * invdy2;\n    const double czz = (zp + zn - 2.0 * center) * invdz2;\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\n__global__ void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                               const double* __restrict__ c,\n                                               const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                               const double invdx2, const double invdy2,\n                                               const double invdz2,\n                                               const double gamma, const double e_AA,\n                                               const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = blockIdx.z * BZ + threadIdx.z;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz) return;\n\n    const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * (static_cast<size_t>(ny) * nx)\n                     + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;\n    const double cv = c[idx];\n    const double lap = laplacianGlobal(c, x, y, z, nx, ny, nz, invdx2, invdy2, invdz2);\n\n    mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)\n            + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv\n            - gamma * lap;\n}\n\n// Cahn-Hilliard update step\n__global__ void cahnHilliardUpdateKernel(double* __restrict__ cnew,\n                                         const double* __restrict__ cold,\n                                         const double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                         const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                         const double dtD,\n                                         const double invdx2, const double invdy2,\n                                         const double invdz2) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = blockIdx.z * BZ + threadIdx.z;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz) return;\n\n    const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * (static_cast<size_t>(ny) * nx)\n                     + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;\n    const double lap = laplacianGlobal(mu, x, y, z, nx, ny, nz, invdx2, invdy2, invdz2);\n    cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dtD * lap;\n}\n\n// Initialize concentration field\nvoid initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {\n    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;\n\n    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {\n        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {\n            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {\n                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);\n                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]\n                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;\n                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));\n                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResult(const if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field\n    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded\n    double minVal = c[0];\n    double maxVal = c[0];\n    for (const auto& val : c) {\n        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);\n        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", minVal, maxVal);\n\n    // Values should generally BX is a multiple of the warp size so that accesses\n// along the contiguous X dimension are fully coalesced.\nconstexpr int BX = 32;\nconstexpr int BY = 2;\nconstexpr int BZ = 2;\n\n// Compute the Laplacian directly from global memory. Neighbouring threads\n// share stencil values through the read-only/L1 cache, and the clamped\n// boundary conditions match the original scalar code exactly.\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianGlobal(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                                  const int x, const int y, const int z,\n                                                  const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                                  const double invdx2, const double invdy2,\n                                                  const double invdz2) {\n    const size_t stride_y = nx;\n    const size_t stride_z = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;\n    const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * stride_z\n                     + static_cast<size_t>(y) * stride_y + x;\n    const double center = f[idx];\n    const double xp = (x < nx - 1) ? f[idx + stride_z] : center;\n    const double zn = (z > 0) ? f[idx - stride_z] : center;\n    const double cxx = (xp + xn - 2.0 * center) * invdx2;\n    const double cyy = (yp + yn - 2.0 * center) * invdy2;\n    const double czz = (zp + zn - 2.0 * center) * invdz2;\n    return cxx + cyy + czz;\n}\n\n// Compute chemical potential\n__global__ void computeChemicalPotentialKernel(double* __restrict__ mu,\n                                               const double* __restrict__ c,\n                                               const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                               const double invdx2, const double invdy2,\n                                               const double invdz2,\n                                               const double gamma, const double coef_c,\n                                               const double coef_c0) {\n    const int x = blockIdx.x * BX + threadIdx.x;\n    const int y = blockIdx.y * BY + threadIdx.y;\n    const int z = blockIdx.z * BZ + threadIdx.z;\n    if (x >= nx || y >= ny || z >= nz) return;\n\n    const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * (static_cast<size_t>(ny) * nx)\n                     + static_cast<size_t>(y) * nx + x;\n    const double cv = c[idx];\n    const double lap = laplacianGlobal(c, x, y, z, nx, ny, nz, invdx2, invdy2, invdz2);\n\n    // Algebraically equivalent to the original expression, with the\n    // parameter-dependent terms folded into two host-computed coefficients.\n    mu[idx] = cv * (coef_c + cv * cv) + coef_c0 - BX is a multiple of the warp size so that accesses\n// along the contiguous X dimension are fully coalesced.\nconstexpr int BX = 32;\nconstexpr int BY = 2;\nconstexpr int BZ = 2;\n\n// Compute the Laplacian directly from global memory. Neighbouring threads\n// share stencil values through the read-only/L1 cache, and the clamped\n// boundary conditions match the original scalar code exactly.\ntemplate <bool UNIT_SPACING>\n__device__ __forceinline__ double laplacianGlobal(const double* __restrict__ f,\n                                                  const int x, const int y, const int z,\n                                                  const int nx, const int ny, const int nz,\n                                                  const double invdx2, const double invdy2,\n                                                  const double invdz2) {\n    const size_t stride_y = nx;\n    const size_t stride_z = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;\n    const size_t idx = static_cast<size_t>(z) * stride_z\n                     + static_cast<size_t>(y) * stride_y + x;\n    const double center = f[idx];\n    const double xp = (x < nx - 1) ? f[idx + 1] : center;\n    const double xn = (x > 0) ? f[idx - 1] : center;\n    const double yp = (y < ny - 1) ? f[idx + stride_y] : center;\n    const double yn = (y > 0) ? f[idx - stride_y] : center;\n    const double zp = (z < nz - 1) ? f[idx + stride_z] : center;\n    const double
